#######################################################################
# MHV9coproducts111.txt — the constants of integration of the nine-loop six-gluon MHV amplitude E^(9) of planar N=4 SYM:
# the values E^{a1,...,ak}(1,1,1) of its coproduct components at (u,v,w) = (1,1,1) for EVERY independent tail (a1,...,ak),
# k = 0,...,6 (weights 18,...,12), in the style of the data file EZMHVcoproducts111.txt released with arXiv:2308.08199 (L. Dixon, Y.-T. Liu) on https://s3df.slac.stanford.edu/people/lance/Cosmic/.
# Companion to MHV9septuples_function.txt (the septuple components as full weight-11 functions) and MHV9constants.txt (the same
# constants reduced to the 72 free ones); the three files together define E^(9)
# as a function with no free parameter.
#######################################################################
# Conventions
#  Letters {a, b, c, mu, mv, mw, yu, yv, yw} = {a-hat, b-hat, c-hat, d-hat, e-hat, f-hat, y_u, y_v, y_w} of arXiv:2308.08199.
#  Tails: E^{a1,...,ak} is the {2L-k,1,...,1} coproduct component with ak the LAST symbol entry, as in EZMHVcoproducts111.txt;
#  the tails listed at each level are the independent ones of the relation tables of MHV9septuples_function.txt (the sets
#  singleindep6, doubleindep21, tripleindep62, quadindep166, quint9indep400, sext9indep892 there); the value for any other tail follows
#  from the relations singlerels3, doublerels33, triplerels127, quadrels392, quint9rels1094, sext9rels2708 of that file (or its
#  substitution routines allsinglesub ... allsextsub).  Tails whose first slot is mu, mv or mw vanish (branch-cut condition), as do
#  tails with an odd number of y letters (parity at (1,1,1)); such tails are listed with the value 0.
#  Normalisation: the BDS-like normalisation of arXiv:2308.08199 with the all-orders constant rho = det(1+K) exp[-zeta_2 Gamma_cusp/2]
#  of eq. (2.27) there, continued to order g^18 (the g^18 term of rho is not printed in that reference and was derived from the kernel).
#  Multiple zeta values: zeta(s1,...,sk) = sum_{n1>n2>...>nk>=1} n1^-s1 n2^-s2 ... nk^-sk (first index on the largest summation
#  variable, the convention of arXiv:2308.08199; zeta(5,3) = 0.0377076730...).  Names: z2 = zeta(2), zN = zeta(N) for odd N,
#  zA_B = zeta(A,B), zA_B_C = zeta(A,B,C), ...; even zetas are written as powers of z2 (zeta(4) = 2/5 z2^2, zeta(6) = 8/35 z2^3, ...),
#  so the names Zeta53, Zeta73, ... of EZMHVcoproducts111.txt are z5_3, z7_3, ... here.  The monomial basis is the one listed in
#  MHV9constants.txt (generators z2, z3, z5, z7, z5_3, z9, z7_3, z11, z5_3_3, z9_3, z6_4_1_1, z13, z7_3_3, z5_5_3, z11_3, z9_5, z5_3_3_3,
#  z15, z9_3_3, z7_5_3, z10_2_1_1_1, z13_3, z11_5, z7_3_3_3, z5_5_3_3, z10_2_2_2, z17, z11_3_3, z9_5_3, z7_7_3, z7_5_5, z5_3_3_3_3,
#  z12_2_1_1_1, z15_3, z13_5, z9_3_3_3, z7_5_3_3, z5_5_5_3, z7_3_5_3, z12_2_2_2, z8_2_2_2_2_2); the printed coefficients depend on this
#  choice of generators, and a change of generator changes the coefficients at that weight by an exact linear transformation.
#  Statement names: EZMHVg9_111 is E^(9)(1,1,1); EZMHVg9c_111[x], EZMHVg9dc_111[x,y], EZMHVg9tc_111[...], EZMHVg9qc_111[...] are the
#  single, double, triple and quadruple components as in EZMHVcoproducts111.txt; EZMHVg9qnc_111[...] (quintuple, weight 13) and
#  EZMHVg9sxc_111[...] (sextuple, weight 12) are new at nine loops.  All coefficients are exact rational numbers; Maple syntax.
#
# The 1,548 values below are linear combinations of 72 free constants (1,173 rational coefficients), related by dihedral symmetry
# and the branch-cut condition.
#######################################################################
